|A shredded or revoked engine holds no key (`key_valid` 0). In automatic mode an AES with `sideload` = 1 starts no block for a new message while the delivered key is not valid; the output in manual mode, and for a key that the engine took before `key_valid` cleared, is not stated.
|What does an engine output after its `key_valid` is cleared in manual mode, or when it took the key at a message start before the clear (no output, an error, or an output under a zero key)? Source: `hw/sys/sep/doc/aes.adoc` (`CTRL_SHADOWED.sideload` states the selection only); `hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (Key Manager Key Delivery); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/aes/doc/theory_of_operation.md` (System Key-Manager Interface); `hw/ip/key_manager/doc/firmware.adoc` ("Key Transfer and Sideload Purge", Crypto Engine Drivers).
|`sep_km_key_transfer_rand_test` grades the automatic-mode cell where its AES bank holds no key: after a new `CTRL_SHADOWED` pair the AES gives no output within W (CHK-AES-NOKEY), and its HMAC witness gives the no-key digest. `sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test` grades the same cell after the wipe: the AES gives no output within W (CHK-SHRED-WITNESS, CHK-NOSHRED-HOST-AES). `sep_km_boot_reset_rand_test` grades the absence of a key on the HMAC witness only: its no-key result is the digest of its software key, which the HMAC text states. Its AES output that differs from the golden of the delivered key, or no output, is logged as `OBS-AES-NOKEY` and is never counted as a pass of a refusal, a shred or a revoke; an AES output that equals the golden where no key is expected fails. It does not claim that the AES gives no output. `sep_crypto_delivered_key_select_rand_test` grades the automatic-mode cell after a shred and a new `CTRL_SHADOWED` pair (CHK-KEYSEL-AES-NOKEY).
|===

[[sep-vplan-phase3-blocked-legs]]
====== Blocked legs of tested features

A tested feature can have one blocked leg. The test grades the rest of the
feature, and the note says how the test treats the blocked leg.

[cols="3,5,6,4",options="header"]
|===
|Area |Item |Blocked leg and open question |Note
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|A write to the full HMAC message FIFO back-pressures the interconnect, and the data is not lost.
|Which write after `STATUS.fifo_full` reads 1 is held. A byte packer sits in front of the FIFO, and the register text does not state how many writes it takes after `fifo_full` reads 1. HMAC: state which write is held. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/hmac/regs/gen/adoc/hmac.adoc` (`STATUS`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/hmac/data/hmac.hjson` (`STATUS.fifo_full`).
|`sep_hmac_fifo_stream_rand_test` logs the held beat (`OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HMAC-BACKPRESSURE`, `OBS-HMAC-BP-HOLD-BEAT`) and grades a hold with no lost data (CHK-HMAC-BACKPRESSURE-HOLD). The leg stops after 16 beats; 16 is a termination bound of the leg, not a spec value, and a DUT that holds no beat by then fails
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|RECOV_ALERT_STS has nine bits cleared by writing zero; the three CMD_STAGE alerts ignore the bad command.
|Bit 12 (`CS_BUS_CMP_ALERT`) needs two equal consecutive genbits words on the software port: an attack pattern with no stated stimulus, and a random repeat is a probabilistic event. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`RECOV_ALERT_STS`).
|Graded leg: bits 0 to 3 (CHK-CSRNG-CTRL-MUBI), bit 4 (CHK-CSRNG-FLAG0), bit 13 (CHK-CSRNG-ACMD-INV, for the reserved values whose bits [2:0] are also reserved; acmd 0x9 is logged only in OBS-CSRNG-ACMD-HI), bit 14 (CHK-EDN-INVSEQ, CHK-CSRNG-SW) and bit 15 (CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT), each with its clear by a write of 0 (`sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`). Bit 12 is not driven. No test logs it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|BOOT_INS_CMD (reset 0x901) and BOOT_GEN_CMD (reset 0xFFF003) must use clen 0 or EDN hangs.
|A `clen` other than 0 hangs EDN, and "Fixing this requires disabling and restarting both EDN and CSRNG": the hang has no bounded observable, so it is not injected. The clen position [7:4] is stated (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:164-169`). Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`BOOT_INS_CMD`, `BOOT_GEN_CMD`).
|Graded leg: the reset values 0x901 and 0xFFF003, decoded as acmd 0x1 and 0x3 with clen 0 (`sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`, CHK-EDN-BOOT-CMD); the boot legs use clen 0 and reach `HW_CMD_STS.BOOT_MODE` = 1 with `AUTO_MODE` = 0 (CHK-EDN-BOOT; no boot acknowledge is graded); no clen injection. No test logs it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|`SW_CMD_STS.CMD_STS` codes 0 to 4 (`csrng.hjson:406-440`; EDN `CMD_STS` links to the same codes, edn.adoc:172-176).
|No sentence states the code of a first instantiate on the CSRNG software instance (0 is given only "If the command is successful"), of a repeated instantiate (the INVALID_CMD_SEQ text names other cases) or of an uninstantiate. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/data/csrng.hjson` (`SW_CMD_STS`).
|Graded leg: EDN 1 after a reserved acmd (`CTRL-EDN-STS`, with CHK-EDN-ACK-ERR); CSRNG 0 on the Generate after a Reseed (CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR), 1 after a reserved acmd (CHK-CSRNG-ACMD-INV; 0x9 logged only in OBS-CSRNG-ACMD-HI) and 4 on the refused Generate (CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT). The other codes are logged (`OBS-EDN-STS`: the EDN success code is stated only for the sequence with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` = true, `doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:457-462`, and the leg runs with it false; `OBS-CSRNG-STS`, `OBS-CSRNG-INVSEQ`, `OBS-EDN-INVSEQ`, `OBS-CSRNG-UNINST`)
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|`GENBITS` delivers data only with `SW_APP_ENABLE` True and the OTP input True; otherwise it reads 0 (csrng.adoc:223-225).
|The data value with `SW_APP_ENABLE` True needs the SEP driver of `otp_en_csrng_sw_app_read` (blocked feature above), and the data is pseudo-random with no stated golden for a fresh seed. `GENBITS_FIPS` is not stated for this flow. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`GENBITS`, `GENBITS_VLD`).
|Graded leg: four `GENBITS` reads return 0 with a Generate pending and `SW_APP_ENABLE` not True (`sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`, CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE). The data reads with `SW_APP_ENABLE` True are not graded. No test logs it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|AES `TRIGGER.DATA_OUT_CLEAR` and `TRIGGER.KEY_IV_DATA_IN_CLEAR` clear the named registers with pseudo-random data, and the engine takes a new key, IV and plaintext after the clear.
|The value after a clear has no exact form: `aes.hjson` (`TRIGGER.DATA_OUT_CLEAR`, `TRIGGER.KEY_IV_DATA_IN_CLEAR`) states "pseudo-random data" only, with no value of DATA_OUT or IV after the clear and no read effect. `STATUS.IDLE` is 0 while the clear runs, and that run ends before a STATUS read can sample it. `CHK-AES-IDLE-RESEED` grades the same `STATUS.IDLE` sentence for the PRNG reseed, which the STATUS poll does sample. A check that the value differs from the value before the clear is a random inequality and passes a partial clear, so it is not graded. AES: state a deterministic effect of each clear trigger that software can read. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/aes/regs/gen/adoc/aes.adoc` (`TRIGGER`, `STATUS`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/aes/data/aes.hjson` (`TRIGGER`, `IV`, `DATA_OUT`).
|`sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test` logs the values after both clears (CHK-AES-CLEAR-OUT is blocked: no exact form) and grades the encryption after the key clear and a reload (CHK-AES-CLEAR-KEY) `sep_aes_reseed_trigger_rand_test` logs `OBS-AES-CLEAR-IV` and `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-AES-CLEAR-OUT` as open questions.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS has one bit each for aes, hmac, kmac, otbn, csrng and edn (plus wdt). The bit is the wrapper's sticky bridge error, latches on a TL-UL error response, is cleared by the matching PERIPH_BUS_ERR_CLEAR bit and ORs into PIC source 43.
|CSRNG and EDN bits of PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS. The spec says the bits latch on a TL-UL error and states no access that makes these wrappers return one; the DRBG adapter answers every CPU access to them, so none reaches the bridge bit. Source: `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc` (`DMA_BUS_ERR_CLEAR`, `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_STATUS`, `PERIPH_BUS_ERR_CLEAR`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (OpenTitan Alert Termination).
|`sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` drives HMAC, KMAC and OTBN with in-extent holes, and AES and WDT with a partial write (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc:63-68`: a byte strobe that misses a byte the register owns)
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|The noise and BIW observe FIFOs discard a word while full and set an overflow bit.
|ESRC: when an observe FIFO is full, is the incoming word or the oldest word the one that is discarded? Source: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`INTR_STATUS`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`).
|`sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test` grades the plateau level, the overflow bit and its write-one clear only, and logs `OBS-OBS-OVF-KEEP` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|ESRC `INTR_STATUS` reports the latched interrupt status of eight sources, and `INTR_ENABLE` enables each source.
|ESRC: does an `INTR_STATUS` bit latch while its `INTR_ENABLE` bit is clear, or does the enable gate the source into the status? Source: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`INTR_STATUS`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`).
|Every ESRC entry that grades a status bit writes its enable bit 1 after each TRNG reset (crypto common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test` logs the status under a clear enable bit and grades nothing on it. FCOV `x_esrc_status_enable` has no bin for the (status 1, enable 0) cells. `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test` logs `OBS-STATUS-E` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|Disabling an ESRC health test with `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` clears its state.
|ESRC: what does `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` read after the repetition test is disabled? The vendored tester holds a count of 1 while inactive. Source: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT`).
|`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` (CHK-HT-ENABLE) grades that the count falls below 100 and below its value before the disable, and logs the value as `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HT-ENABLE-REPCOUNT-VALUE`. FCOV `cp_disable_clears` has no graded bin. `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` logs `OBS-HT-REPCOUNT-AFTER` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|With whitening on, the SHA-256 whitener conditions the BIW stream, and a 32-bit health-test failure after boot does not stop the module.
|ESRC: after the 32-bit health test fails after `BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, does the whitener keep taking input words? Source: `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Health Testing, SHA-256 Whitening).
|`sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test` (CHK-HT-PRESHA) grades the exact 16-to-8 count when at least 64 input words pass; a trace with fewer input words logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HT-PRESHA-NOPUSH` and the missing check line fails the leaf
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|The SHA-256 whitener accumulates 16 BIW words into one block and emits 8 digest words.
|ESRC: does the whitener take every BIW word while it hashes a block, or can a BIW word reach the BIW observe FIFO and not the whitener? Source: `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (SHA-256 Whitening); `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`SHA256_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`).
|`sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test` (CHK-COND-SHA) grades the exact count on the whitener input strobe `esrc_whiten_push_o`, not on the BIW observe count, and logs both as `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-SHA-INTAKE`
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|A second write with a different value to a shadowed register sets the update error. `aes.hjson` states for AES `CTRL_SHADOWED` and `CTRL_AUX_SHADOWED` that a read clears the phase tracking, so the next write is a first write.
|KMAC: does a read of a shadowed register between the two writes restart the write sequence? `kmac.hjson` does not say. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/gen/adoc/kmac.adoc` (`STATUS`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/data/kmac.hjson`.
|`sep_crypto_shadow_update_err_rand_test` issues no read between the two writes of a graded pair. It logs one KMAC `CFG_SHADOWED` pair with a read between the writes as `OBS-SHADOW-KMAC-RDGAP` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|The ABR sequencer parks with STATUS.VALID set after a result, and STATUS.READY re-asserts after a zeroize or a reset.
|ABR: does STATUS.VALID clear when READY re-asserts after an ABR reset? After a zeroize, `hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/doc/abr-mldsa-driver.adoc` (the driver's zeroize step) states STATUS reads READY alone. Source: `hw/sys/sep/doc/adams_bridge.adoc`; `integration/rdl/sep/abr_reg.rdl` (`STATUS`).
|`sep_abr_zeroize_latency_test` (CHK-ABR-ZEROIZE-READY) grades VALID clear after a zeroize; `sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test` grades READY and logs VALID. `sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test` logs `OBS-ABR-VALID` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|An ML-DSA zeroize returns the ABR sequencer to RESET, and `STATUS.READY` re-asserts after it.
|ABR: how many clock cycles does a zeroize take, from the `ZEROIZE` write to `STATUS.READY`? Source: `hw/sys/sep/doc/adams_bridge.adoc`; `integration/rdl/sep/abr_reg.rdl` (`MLDSA_CTRL.ZEROIZE`).
|`sep_abr_zeroize_latency_test` logs the engine-side and bus-side counts as `OBS-ABR-ZEROIZE-CYCLES` and grades nothing on them
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|Invalid encodings map to safe values: MODE to AES_NONE, KEY_LEN to AES_256, OPERATION to AES_ENC, PRNG_RESEED_RATE to PER_1, PHASE to GCM_INIT, NUM_VALID_BYTES to 16.
|Readback of an invalid encoding. The RDL does not say whether the readback shows the written or the mapped value. Source: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/aes/regs/gen/adoc/aes.adoc` (`CTRL_SHADOWED`).
|`sep_aes_encoding_inject_rand_test` grades behaviour and logs the readback. `sep_aes_encoding_inject_rand_test` logs `OBS-AES-ENC-RB` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-spi-dma,SPI and DMA>>
|STATUS reset value
|The RDL reset column states 0x0 for READY, TXEMPTY and RXEMPTY (`spi_controller.adoc`, STATUS). The Phase 1 and Phase 2 reset check requires them set. Which one is the contract?
|Effect: The tests read STATUS at run time and never compare READY, TXEMPTY or RXEMPTY with a literal. `sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test` logs the three bits in `OBS-SPI-STATUS-RESET` as an open question, and grades TXSTALL, RXSTALL, TXWM and RXWM at their RDL reset 0x0 (CHK-SPI-STATUS-RESET).
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-spi-dma,SPI and DMA>>
|SPI RX watermark meaning
|Does RXWM mean "reaches" or "exceeds" the threshold (`spi_controller.adoc`, CONTROL, STATUS, EVENT_ENABLE)?
|Effect: STATUS.RXWM at equality is never graded (`sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test`, `sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test`). `sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test` keeps C/4 at or below RX_WATERMARK in its DMA runs, and grades the DMA trigger at RXQD equal to RX_WATERMARK, where `spi.adoc` (DMA Trigger) states `RXQD >= RX_WATERMARK` (CHK-HS-TRIG-EQ) `sep_spi_fifo_stall_watermark_rand_test` logs `OBS-SPI-WM` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-spi-dma,SPI and DMA>>
|Partial strobe merge
|What is the merge rule for a partial strobe inside a half (`spi.adoc`, Features)?
|Effect: Partial strobes are not driven (`sep_spi_ot_host_csr_irq_rand_test`) No test logs it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-spi-dma,SPI and DMA>>
|Short or oversized final chunk
|What does a short final chunk or a chunk larger than the total do (`secure_dma.adoc`, CHUNK_DATA_SIZE)?
|Effect: Not driven. No test logs it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-spi-dma,SPI and DMA>>
|INCREMENT=0 with WRAP=0 and two or more chunks
|Where does a later chunk start when INCREMENT=0 and WRAP=0, given "not changed" for INCREMENT=0 and "contiguous ascending addresses" for WRAP=0 (`secure_dma.adoc`, SRC_CONFIG, DST_CONFIG)?
|Effect: The graded cells drive one chunk only (`sep_dma_transfer_matrix_rand_test`). The same test logs a two-chunk copy from a fixed source as `OBS-DMA-FIXED-CHUNK` as an open question.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-spi-dma,SPI and DMA>>
|DMA interrupt clear write timing
|When does the clear write of CLEAR_INTR_SRC land relative to the trigger and the chunk, and how many clear writes does one trigger give (`secure_dma.adoc`, CLEAR_INTR_SRC, INTR_SRC_ADDR_0)?
|Effect: The clock and the count of the clear writes are logged only (`OBS-HS-CLR`, `sep_spi_dma_handshake_rand_test`). The bus encoding is graded: with CLEAR_INTR_BUS bit 0 = 1 the clear write reaches INTR_SRC_ADDR_0 on the DMA master (`sep-programming.adoc:800-803`, CHK-HS-CLR)
|===

[[sep-vplan-phase3-unstated]]
====== Unstated cells that block no feature

<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>:

* The `ERR_BITS` and `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE` bits other than the named integrity bit after an OTBN IMEM or DMEM integrity error: "a non-zero value that describes the error" does not fix them. `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` grades the named bits and logs the full values (`OBS-OTBN-INTG-CAUSE`).
* The response code of a host read of an OTBN memory while OTBN executes, and the `ERR_BITS` and `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE` bits other than the named ones after a software error of a drawn kind or an illegal bus access: the text states that the read returns zero and is a fatal error, and no code or other bit. `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test` logs them (`OBS-OTBN-ILLEGAL-BUS-CAUSE`, `OBS-OTBN-SWERR-KIND-CAUSE`).
* The loss or repeat of noise samples when the sample clock runs near two `clk_i` periods: no sentence sets a maximum sample rate. `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test` logs the lost and repeated bits (`OBS-OBS-FAST`).

<<sep-vplan-phase3-mailbox-irq-pic,Mailbox, IRQ, PIC>>:

* The response of a write of 0 to a W 1P clear register is not stated beyond "write 1 to clear". `sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` grades that 0 leaves the latch set and does not grade the response code.

<<sep-vplan-phase3-fabric-remap,Fabric and remap>>:

* The response of a Reserved, Past Extent or Hole in Extent cell as seen by the CPU LSU or the DMA. `hw/sys/sep/doc/memory_map.adoc` states the cells for a 32-bit access as seen at the inbound AXI port, and says what the SEP CPU sees only for the boot ROM, reset control, ICCM, DCCM, PIC and AP/STEE rows. The fabric leaves grade the reset-control gaps, tails and past-extent cells from the LSU against the table, because the specification states the CPU view for those rows. For every other row, the fabric leaves grade the cells through the System Interface and log the LSU value. The response of the fabric to a DMA-master access at a reserved window is not stated either, so no address can stand in for the non-OKAY response control of the outbound responder. The non-OKAY leg of the DMA host-path latch in `sep_cpu_bridge_fault_matrix_rand_test` uses that control, an error inject that the test adds (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks,Hooks that the tests add>>).
* The code that the initiator receives for a split 64-bit TRNG-window write whose last port write errs, and for a 64-bit TRNG-window read whose two port reads answer different codes. `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc` states only the case of an earlier error and a last OKAY. `sep_fabric_deadspace_decode_test` logs the codes (`OBS-TRNG-SPLIT-PREC`).
* The response and read data of a 1-, 2- or 8-byte access to a Reserved, Past Extent or Hole in Extent cell. The table states the cells only for a 32-bit access. The row leaf grades AxSIZE 2 in both halves of the bus and logs AxSIZE 3.

<<sep-vplan-phase3-mem-boot-reset,Memory, boot, reset>>:

* The assertion timing of `sep_intermediate_reset_n`, and so of `sep_reset_n`, after the `rst_ni` fall. `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc` (SEP Reset Controller) states that the eFuse wrapper synchronizes the AND of the boot terms into `sep_intermediate_reset_n` and that `u_sep_reset_sync` asserts its input asynchronously, and gives no assertion behaviour for the first synchronizer. `sep_cold_reset_phase_test` logs the delay as `OBS-RST-ASSERT` and grades the release only.
* Whether the watchdog count and the bite request advance while `clk_i` is stopped. `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc` and `doc/trm/src/clock_domains.adoc` state that the watchdog clock is independent of the system clock so that the watchdog can detect system clock failures, and no sentence says what the count or the bite request does with `clk_i` stopped. `sep_timer_clock_contract_test` logs the count tap and the bite request against the `clk_wdt_i` edges (`OBS-WDT-CLKSTOP`) and grades nothing; it controls that `clk_i` is stopped (`CTRL-CLKI-STOPPED`).
* When the watchdog reset asserts in the watchdog timer domain. `hw/sys/sep/doc/clk_rst.adoc:62` states that its release is synchronized to `clk_wdt_i` and gives no assertion timing. `sep_timer_clock_contract_test` logs the `clk_i` edge at which `wdt_timer_rst_req_o` falls in a `wdt_rst_ni_i` pulse (`OBS-BITE-CLEAR-EDGE`) and grades only that the request holds for one `clk_i` cycle and falls within a logged bound of 4 `clk_wdt_i` periods (CHK-BITE-CLEAR).

<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>:

* Whether every Class-1 shadow word carries the `_n0_scan` name. `hw/sys/sep/doc/attack_countermeasures.adoc` states that a nested `_n0_scan` name remains Class 1, which `sep_scan_mode_state_hold_test` grades (CHK-SCAN-N0-CLASS1), and no sentence states the reverse. The leaf logs the Class-1 words that the `_n0_scan` storage lacks (`OBS-SCAN-N0-COMPLETE`).

<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager vault>>:

* The reserved bits of CMD_KEY_REVOKE, CMD_KEY_TRANSFER and CMD_ENGINE_SHRED, the transfer mask 0 and the shred mask 0. (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/firmware.adoc, sections "0x23 - CMD_KEY_REVOKE", "0x24 - CMD_KEY_TRANSFER", "0x25 - CMD_ENGINE_SHRED".) `sep_km_command_set_rand_test` sends one frame of each kind with a reserved bit or an empty mask set and logs the code and argument (`OBS-CMD-ARG-EDGE`), without a verdict. The other tests keep the reserved bits clear.
* The return code of CMD_KEY_REVOKE for an unavailable handle, and the return code of any command for a null or never-issued handle. (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/firmware.adoc, section "0x23 - CMD_KEY_REVOKE".) A CMD_KEY_TRANSFER of a revoked handle is a failure (section "Key Transfer and Sideload Purge") and `sep_km_command_set_rand_test` grades it (CHK-CLOSED).
* The return argument of CMD_KEY_GENERATE when both REQ_SIZE and DEST_VALID are invalid. (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/firmware.adoc, section "0x22 - CMD_KEY_GENERATE".) `sep_km_command_protocol_rand_test` logs it and does not grade it.
* The read response of a write-only register (KM_WRITE_DATA and the key-share registers). `sep_km_crossbar_efuse_window_rand_test` logs it and does not grade it.
* The TIMEOUT_ERR and COUNT_BAD results when a stream error and a timeout expiry fall in one cycle (STREAM_ERR set is stated). (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc, section "Errors and reset".) `sep_km_drbg_sampler_rand_test` logs them and does not grade them.
* The precedence of the handover-disabled and zero-size conditions of CMD_SRAM_EXEC. (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/firmware.adoc, section "0x12 - CMD_SRAM_EXEC".)
* The CMD_SRAM_LOAD_EXEC image ceiling value. `sep_km_handover_rand_test` grades sizes at or above the SRAM size and logs the rest.
* The read value of a locked KPV_SCRAMBLER_KEY. (hw/ip/key_manager/regs/gen/adoc/km_kpv.adoc, sections "CTRL[64]", "KPV_SCRAMBLER_KEY".) `sep_km_kpv_rand_test` logs it (`OBS-KPV-ACCESS`, `case=scr_key_locked`) and does not grade it.
* The value of OTP_EFUSE_REMAP_BASE. (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc.) `sep_km_crossbar_efuse_window_rand_test` grades that the base is constant.
* The OTP readout value before the sense completes, and the length of the OTP change suppression window. (hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc, section "Fuse Sensing and Boot Sequencing"; hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc, section "OTP Inputs and Readout".) `sep_km_otp_interface_rand_test` logs the KM identity inputs from the release to the sense-done rise (`OBS-OTP-PRESENSE`) and does not grade them.
* Whether SEP_IRQ_STATUS.flushed_by_km sets at the boot flush of the ROM. (hw/ip/key_manager/regs/gen/adoc/km_mailbox_sep.adoc, section "SEP_IRQ_STATUS".) `sep_km_boot_reset_rand_test` logs it per boot (`OBS-BOOT-FLUSHED-BY-KM`) and does not grade it.
* The byte-wise permutation (perm8) of the KM SRAM scrambler and the address permutations (perm10, perm13) are not tabulated in hw/ip/scrambler/doc/architecture.adoc. No cipher golden exists for the KM SRAM stored word. `sep_km_sram_scrambler_key_rand_test` grades the KM SRAM by key, address and bijection, and `sep_km_kpv_rand_test` grades the cipher on the KPV path (whole-word permutation) and logs the KPV cells read after a cold reset, because the stored words then lie at the indices that perm10 selects.

[[sep-vplan-phase3-noobs]]
===== Features with no observation point

A disposition says what is done about the missing observation. `ADD-PORT`: a
testbench port, drive or hook that the bench lacks would give the observation,
and the DUT does not change. `ACCEPT-PROBE`: a read-only probe or hierarchical
reference, or a firmware CSR read, is the observation. A probe or reference that
`hw/sys/sep/dv/docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` does not list is an observation tap the
test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list, and a check on it is
executable. `PERMANENT-HOLE`: no observation exists at SEP level, so no check can
fail on the contract.

A read-only probe, tap or reference is an observation tap and is not PROPOSED.
A port, drive, inject or hook that exists in `hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` or that
`SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` lists is not PROPOSED, and the row says so. An error inject
and a drive of a port of the `sep` module are added hooks and are not PROPOSED
(<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>). A drive, force or deposit of a net inside `sep`
for a purpose other than error injection is PROPOSED.
<<sep-vplan-phase3-proposed>> lists each observation tap, each added hook and
each PROPOSED item with the tests that use it. A check that needs a PROPOSED
item is blocked (<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>).

[cols="3,4,4,2,8",options="header"]
|===
|Area |Feature |Missing observation |Disposition |Reason
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-crypto-entropy,Crypto and entropy>>
|Secret keys travel only on the private Key Manager AXI4-Lite key buses. Host data and CSR traffic uses the separate AXI4 bus from the SEP fabric.
|-

|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Reachability versus command class
|-
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|`sep_lcc_feat_ctrl_state_matrix_rand_test` grades the feature-bit half (SECURE_TM is not a term).
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Class 2b scan naming of the fuse MMRs
|-
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|Scan-membership audit.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Absence of an escalation or wipe port
|-
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|A port-list elaboration audit closes it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Absence of a memory-scramble-key interface
|-
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|Elaboration audit.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Scan test versus dump operation class
|-
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|Admission belongs to the adopter DFT integration (`hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc`, Protection While Active). The RTL simulation has no scan chain, so no OCAH port can report the command class, as for secure scan chain command admission.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Program and read commands that reach the bank port after the guard
|The command at `efuse_shim_command_req_o` after the guard
|ACCEPT-PROBE
|The tap is an observation tap that the owning tests add to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. Checks that read it: every command-count and read-command statement (CHK-DEMOTE-OTP, CHK-TRANSIENT-NOBURN, CHK-PROG-LC, the read statements of CHK-IF-CMD and CHK-IF-CTRL, CHK-IF-PRESENSE, CHK-LOCK-CELL, CHK-GUARD-NOPULSE, CHK-STM-PROG, CHK-STM-PROG-CTL, CHK-JTAG and the class C command statement of `sep_sec_dis_timing_rand_test`). The OTP image diff reads the observation tap `otp_bank_probe_o`. A direct OTP word read stands in for "no program command" only where the read interface is not under test.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Bank access start after the guard
|The APB `psel` of the shim request to the bank model (`efuse_bank_psel_o`)
|ACCEPT-PROBE
|The tap is an observation tap that the owning test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. CHK-GUARD-NOPULSE of `sep_efuse_lock_slot_matrix_rand_test` reads it on every clock beside `efuse_shim_cmd_valid_o`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Reset state of the 24 token word registers
|The X value of an unreset token word array
|ACCEPT-PROBE
|The token word tap is an observation tap that the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. CHK-NORESET of `sep_efuse_token_channel_rand_test` reads it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|`jtag_id_i` in the shadow words
|A drive of `jtag_id_i[31:1]`; no shadow word carries the ID
|ADD-PORT
|`tb_top` drives the port from the TB input `jtag_id_i`; an undriven or unknown value drives 0. The drive is a top-port drive, an added hook (<<sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks>>). CHK-JTAGID of `sep_efuse_field_layout_rand_test` uses it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Comparator copy of a token digest match
|The copy select (copy 1 and copy 2) at the `token_cmp_fault` injection port
|ADD-PORT
|The copy select is an error inject, an added hook. The copy 1 and copy 2 cells of `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test` use it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Digest latch of the RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS channels
|The sticky digest and valid bit, and a force of the latch test enable (the existing probes cover RMA_SIP only)
|ADD-PORT
|The probes are observation taps that the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The test-enable inject is an error inject, an added hook. The RMA_CHIPLET and SEC_DIS cells of CHK-LATCH-FREEZE in `sep_efuse_token_fault_matrix_test` use the inject. The RMA_SIP cell is the control.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|JTAG lifecycle filter under a lifecycle integrity error
|An injection at the decoder input of the JTAG lifecycle filter (`jtag_filter_lc_sigint_inject_i`) and a read-only probe of the integrity-error flag at its decoder output (`jtag_filter_lc_sigint_err_probe_o`)
|ADD-PORT
|The inject is an error inject, an added hook. The probe is an observation tap that the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. CHK-JTAG-SIGINT of `sep_lcc_state_codeword_rand_test` uses the inject.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Sense response with the status bit set
|A status bit on the sense response of one bank-model word (`efuse_sense_status_inject_i` with `efuse_sense_status_word_i`)
|ADD-PORT
|The inject is an error inject, an added hook. CHK-SENSE-STATUS of `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test` uses it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-efuse-lifecycle,eFuse, lifecycle>>
|Program interface under a withheld shim response
|A hold of the shim response of a program request while the input is high (`efuse_shim_resp_hold_i`)
|ADD-PORT
|The hold is an error inject, an added hook. CHK-PROG-LIFE and OBS-PROG-TIMEOUT of `sep_efuse_shim_response_fault_test` use it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Cold-path reset synchronizer
|The reset synchronizer structure of the cold path (asynchronous assert, synchronous release, no hold counter)
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The structure has no port. Its effect (a cold reset clears both domains) is graded in `sep_km_kmcsr_reset_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Conditioned reset nets
|The two conditioned reset nets and the asynchronous clear of the warm hold counter
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|No port or register shows the nets. The effect (a cold reset also resets the warm domain) is graded in `sep_km_kmcsr_reset_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Crossbar state at warm reset
|The crossbar channel state at a warm reset, and the initiator of every access on the KM crossbar
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The text states what the reset does not guarantee for an external transaction. No pass or fail follows. The sole-master property is structural.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Address order of KPV and scrambler-key writes
|The address order of KPV slot accesses and of the SRAM and KPV scrambler key writes (KPV and scrambler-key part of the engine write-order feature)
|ACCEPT-PROBE
|KM-BUS can show the sequence. The specification states only "sequential" and "index order" with no address pattern to compare. No check exists. `sep_km_key_transfer_rand_test` grades the bank order of a multi-engine transfer (CHK-ENG-ORDER) and logs the per-bank share writes; the address set of the share writes is its Planned check CHK-DELIV. `KM-BUS` is an observation tap.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Sampler FSM state
|The sampler FSM state
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|STATUS has no state field. The state effects (COUNT_GOOD, errors, SLVERR) are graded in `sep_km_drbg_sampler_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Fatal codes -11, -15, -16
|A stimulus for ebreak, stack overflow and shred range inside the ROM
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The production ROM contains none of these faults and the host cannot raise them. Code -12 is blocked (spurious interrupt code). Code -1 is graded in `sep_km_wipe_reset_test`, and code -14 in `sep_km_handover_rand_test` and `sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test` (CHK-MAINCTX-FATAL). Codes -2 to -8, -13, -17 and -18 are graded in `sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test` with the error injects HK-IRQSET and HK-PAR, and code -8 also on an organic DRBG stall (CHK-DRBG-STALL). Codes -9 and -10 are graded in `sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test` (CHK-CPU-TRAP) with the error inject HK-CPUIRQ on the PicoRV32 interrupt lines 1 and 2.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Shred count out of range
|A shred count outside the range
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|Current callers use compile-time-bounded counts. The path needs a firmware defect or corrupted state.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Mailbox overflow and underflow codes
|A stimulus for a KM-side mailbox overflow or underflow in the production ROM (recoverable codes -3 and -4, and the outbound-overflow and inbound-underflow legs of the mailbox ISR order)
|ADD-PORT
|The ROM never writes a full FIFO or reads an empty one. An injected Key Manager mailbox error (a set of KM_IRQ_STATUS.outbound_overflow or inbound_underflow) would make the codes and the ISR order observable. No inject of `tb_top.sv` and no added hook of a Phase 3 entry provides it. The codes -2 and -5 are graded in `sep_km_command_protocol_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Co-asserted fatal sources 1, 15, 16, 17
|A stimulus for EBREAK or illegal instruction, the mailbox, the ABR shared key and the spurious interrupt as fatal co-asserted sources
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|These are CPU-internal or non-fatal sources. The eleven KMCSR fatal sources are graded in pairs in `sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test`, and the bus error (source 2) with one KMCSR source in the same clock in CHK-CPU-TRAP.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Key registry metadata
|The key registry metadata (base slot, word count, CRC, policy, reverse map)
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The registry is firmware RAM in the scrambled SRAM. Its effects are graded: a revoked handle is unusable in `sep_km_command_set_rand_test` (CHK-CLOSED) and its slots are erased in `sep_km_key_vault_alloc_rand_test` (CHK-REVOKE), policy refuses transfers in `sep_km_key_transfer_rand_test` (CHK-REFUSE), and the CRC check in `sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Firmware PRNG state
|The xoshiro128++ state and the shuffle index array
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The state is seeded from DRBG words and written into the scrambled SRAM. No output relation can be checked without the scrambler key and the seed.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Adopter DFT test-command class
|The admitted test-command class of the adopter DFT controller
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The adopter integration owns command admission (hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc, sections "Wipe State", "Scan and Scan-Dump Protection"). The `secure_tm_o` port is sampled in `sep_km_otp_interface_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|CPU interrupt mask restore and RX and TX indices
|The CPU interrupt mask restore and the RX and TX buffer indices
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|Internal CPU and firmware RAM state. The order and no-loss behavior are graded in `sep_km_command_protocol_rand_test`.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|DMA access initiator at the crypto target
|The initiator tag and address of a DMA access at the crypto target
|ACCEPT-PROBE
|The check needs an initiator tag at the crypto target and a firmware-driven secure DMA read of the KM aperture in run mode `cpu`. The DMA tests own this leg; the Key Manager scope records no credit for it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|Isolation of the AES, OTBN and eFuse paths
|The isolation request and acknowledge of the AES, OTBN and eFuse paths, the drain of accepted transactions and the order against the warm reset
|ADD-PORT
|Only three isolation probes exist (HMAC, KMAC, ABR). `sep_km_kmcsr_reset_rand_test` grades those three. The reset controller tests own the sequencer.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|KM SRAM scratch locations of key material
|KM SRAM scratch locations of key material
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|The scratch lives in the scrambled SRAM at addresses the specification does not state. A test cannot search the array for plaintext.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|The allocator never uses a read-locked, sealed or retired slot.
|A stimulus that sets seal or lock_use on a slot before an allocation
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|No ROM flow and no host command sets seal or lock_use before an allocation. The allocator capacity oracle (CHK-CAP of `sep_km_key_vault_alloc_rand_test`) covers the live-key and revoked-slot cases only.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|A slot-write error during CMD_KEY_LOAD destroys the handle registration, keeps the earlier vault writes and does not reuse the handle.
|A failure of one KPV slot write
|ADD-PORT
|The slot-write failure needs an injected KPV write failure. No inject of `tb_top.sv` and no added hook of a Phase 3 entry provides it, and no entry grades the contract.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|CMD_KEY_TRANSFER keeps the earlier engine writes after a driver failure.
|A failure of a later engine key write of the mask, after an earlier bank is written
|ADD-PORT
|`sep_km_fault_inject_rand_test` (CHK-XFER-STOP) grades the stop at the first driver failure with a legal stimulus: a key write to HMAC while HMAC is in its software reset fails, and no later bank is written. The keep of an earlier bank needs the failure at a later bank; no entry grades it.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|The OTP change interrupt flow of the ROM.
|The source is not enabled at boot, so the flow is dormant in the ROM.
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|No stimulus reaches a dormant flow without a firmware change.
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-key-manager,Key Manager>>
|The allocator starts at a DRBG-selected slot.
|The start slot of an allocation
|PERMANENT-HOLE
|No net shows the DRBG-selected start slot. The capacity oracle does not depend on it.
|===

[[sep-vplan-phase3-proposed]]
===== Observation taps, added hooks and proposed drives

This section has three lists. <<sep-vplan-phase3-obs-taps>> lists the read-only
observations that Phase 3 tests use and that `hw/sys/sep/dv/docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`
does not list: each is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH
observation-probe list. <<sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks>> lists the error injects
and the top-port drives that Phase 3 tests use and that `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` does
not list: each is an added hook that the test adds to the inject exceptions or
to the HDL Top port table of `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. It also lists the existing
bench hooks of `hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` that Phase 3 tests use and that
`SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` does not list. An observation tap, an added hook and an
existing bench hook are not PROPOSED, so a check or bin that needs only these is
executable (<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>). <<sep-vplan-phase3-proposed-drives>> lists the
PROPOSED items: each drives, forces or deposits into a net inside the `sep`
module for a purpose other than error injection, and it is not in
`SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` and not in `hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv`. No item changes DUT
RTL. A check that needs a PROPOSED item is blocked: it is not a pass and takes no
credit. An item with two uses is one row in each list that applies. A preload
(<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>) is part of the approved t=0 image load and is in
none of the three lists; <<sep-vplan-km-proposed>> names the preloads of the Key
Manager tests.

Notes on these lists:

* `sep_cpu_ctrl_err_pulse_probe_o` (Mailbox, IRQ, PIC) and `dma_bus_err_latch_probe_o` (SPI and DMA) give the same observation of the DMA host-path latch.
* The Key Manager contracts of a partial `CMD_KEY_LOAD` failure, a partial `CMD_KEY_TRANSFER` failure, a Key Manager mailbox overflow or underflow, and a Key Manager CPU interrupt line need four injects: a KPV slot-write failure, an engine key-write failure, a Key Manager mailbox error and a CPU interrupt line. No Phase 3 entry uses them, so they are not in this list (<<sep-vplan-phase3-noobs>>).

|Key Manager FCOV sampler taps (`KM-COV`)
|Sampler taps
|Read-only nets `km_sram_lock`, `km_irq_entry`, `km_kpv_scrambler`, `km_mbx_fifo`, `km_mbx_regs`, `km_drbg_sampler`, `km_cpu_trap`, `km_cpu_irq_active`, `km_cpu_insn_opcode`, `km_cpu_irq_lines`, `km_cpu_axi`, `km_axi_err`, `km_err_flags`, `km_wipe`, `km_otp_input`, `km_rom_rsp`, the `addr` field of `km_rom_req`, the `rdata` and `rparity` fields of `km_sram_mem_if`, `km_smp_sink`, `km_cpu_pcpi`, `aes_sideload_key`, `km_err_out_port`, `efuse_shadow_sensed` and `efuse_req_err` (<<sep-vplan-km-proposed>>). The sampler reads `efuse_req_err` through the `req_err_i` port and `efuse_shadow_sensed` through the `stored_shadow_i` port of the eFuse and lifecycle sampler taps: the Key Manager adds no port for them.
|The owners of the groups of the Key Manager section of `SEP_FCOV.adoc`
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-mem-boot-reset,Memory, boot, reset>>
|VeeR CPU AXI protocol checkers (`u_cpu_lsu_axi_sva`, `u_cpu_ifu_axi_sva`, `u_cpu_sb_axi_sva`, `u_cpu_tcm_axi_sva`)
|Monitor (`ocah_axi_sva`)
|The AXI4 rules of `ocah_axi_sva` (reset, handshake, X, burst, ID and response rules) on the LSU, IFU and debug system-bus masters at the core side of `sep_cpu`, before the alias remap and the demuxes, and on the DMA slave port into the ICCM and DCCM. `aresetn` is SEP `rst_ni` on every port. The debug system-bus checker arms on the edge after the first edge that samples its own reset `dbg_rstb_i` low; a `+cpu_boot` run that never arms it fails. Each named test carries `+axi_sva_need_<port>` and fails when that port's checker graded no AW or AR handshake. Enabled under `+cpu_boot` on the full-CPU target only. Protocol, not data. VCS only: the SEP Verilator targets pass no `--assert`.
|Every test of the `cpu` group; the IFU port is driven by the boot ROM fetch tests (`sep_boot_rom_smoke_test`), the debug system-bus master by `sep_cpu_reset_entry_test`, the DMA slave port by `sep_dma_basic_test` (CHK-ICCM)
|<<sep-vplan-phase3-fabric-remap,Fabric and remap>>
|SEP AXI master protocol checkers outside the CPU (`u_dma_axi_sva`, `u_smn_out_axi_sva`, `u_smc_out_axi_sva`, `u_ext_out_axi_sva`)
|Monitor (`ocah_axi_sva`)
|The AXI4 rules of `ocah_axi_sva` (reset, handshake, X, burst, ID and response rules) on the secure DMA master into the local crossbar, the SMN outbound port, the SMC aperture port and the external aperture into the IP integration. On the SMN and SMC ports the testbench responders answer B and R, so the checkers also grade them; on the external port the testbench responder answers under `+sep_ext_port_responder`, and the DECERR error slave of the IP integration answers without it. `aresetn` is SEP `rst_ni`; the DMA engine and the SMN and SMC request logic reset on `sep_reset_n`, and a SEP reset from software or JTAG is outside `aresetn`. Each named test carries `+axi_sva_need_<port>` and fails when that port's checker graded no AW or AR handshake. Compiled in every build and run mode. Protocol, not data. VCS only: the SEP Verilator targets pass no `--assert`.
|Every test; the DMA port is driven by `sep_dma_basic_test`, the SMN outbound port by `sep_fabric_outbound_route_attr_test`, the SMC aperture port by `sep_fabric_smc_route_test`, the external aperture by `sep_fabric_extension_port_window_test`
|===

[[sep-vplan-phase3-added-hooks]]
====== Hooks that the tests add

Each item is an added hook (<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>): an error inject, which
the test adds to the inject exceptions of `hw/sys/sep/dv/docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`,
or a top-port drive, which the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top
section of `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. An item of the kind "Existing bench hook" exists
in `hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv` and `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc` does not list it: it is
approved, and the test that uses it adds its row to `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`
(<<sep-vplan-credit-rules>>). An item of the kind "Existing bench hook, listed"
exists in `tb_top.sv` and in the inject exceptions of `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`; the
list names it for its users only, and the test adds no row. An added hook and an
existing bench hook are not PROPOSED and block no check, leg or bin. The column "Port or inject point" names the `sep` port of a top-port
drive (`hw/sys/sep/rtl/sep.sv`) and the point of an error inject. The memory
macros, the eFuse integration and the external TRNG source are in
`hw/top/sep_ip_integration.sv`, outside the `sep` module. The column "Tests that
use it" names the entries whose checks need the hook.

[cols="2,3,2,5,3,5",options="header"]
